+package com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.api
+
+import java.io.File
+import java.util.zip.ZipFile
+import javax.net.ssl.HostnameVerifier
+import javax.net.ssl.TrustManager
+import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
+import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
+import org.junit.Test
+
+/**
+ * No code path accepts a certificate without a check (docs/PRD.md S3): no
+ * trust-all trust manager and no host name verifier anywhere, in the app,
+ * its debug build, its tests or the scripts. The only trust manager is
+ * [PinningTrustManager], and only [SharedHttpClient] installs it.
+ */
+class NoTrustAllTest {
+
+    @Test
+    fun sharedClientUsesThePinningTrustManagerAndOkHttpsHostNameCheck() {
+        val client = SharedHttpClient.instance
+
+        assertTrue(client.x509TrustManager is PinningTrustManager)
+        assertEquals("okhttp3.internal.tls.OkHostnameVerifier", client.hostnameVerifier.javaClass.name)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun noCompiledAppClassButThePinningTrustManagerIsATrustManagerOrHostnameVerifier() {
+        val classes = appClasses()
+        assertTrue("the scan found the app's classes", classes.any { it.name == "com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.MainActivity" })
+
+        val trustManagers = classes.filter { TrustManager::class.java.isAssignableFrom(it) }.map { it.name }
+        val verifiers = classes.filter { HostnameVerifier::class.java.isAssignableFrom(it) }.map { it.name }
+
+        assertEquals(listOf(PinningTrustManager::class.java.name), trustManagers)
+        assertEquals(emptyList<String>(), verifiers)
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * Kotlin compiles a lambda for a Java interface without a class of its
+     * own, so the sources are read too: every source set and the scripts.
+     */
+    @Test
+    fun noSourceOrScriptBuildsAnotherTrustPath() {
+        val module = moduleDirectory()
+        val root = checkNotNull(module.parentFile)
+        val sources = File(module, "src").walkTopDown()
+            .filter { it.isFile && (it.extension == "kt" || it.extension == "java") && it.name != "NoTrustAllTest.kt" }
+            .toList()
+        val scripts = File(root, "scripts").walkTopDown().filter { it.isFile }.toList()
+        assertTrue("the scan found the sources", sources.any { it.name == "SharedHttpClient.kt" })
+        assertTrue("the scan found the scripts", scripts.any { it.name == "instrumented-tests.sh" })
+
+        val offenders = sources.flatMap { file ->
+            val text = file.readText()
+            SOURCE_RULES.filter { (pattern, allowedIn) -> pattern.containsMatchIn(text) && file.name !in allowedIn }
+                .map { (pattern, _) -> "${file.relativeTo(root)} matches ${pattern.pattern}" }
+        } + scripts.flatMap { file ->
+            val text = file.readText()
+            SCRIPT_RULES.filter { it.containsMatchIn(text) }.map { "${file.relativeTo(root)} matches ${it.pattern}" }
+        }
+
+        assertTrue("Only PinningTrustManager may decide trust:\n" + offenders.joinToString("\n"), offenders.isEmpty())
+    }
+
+    private fun appClasses(): List<Class<*>> {
+        val location = File(SharedHttpClient::class.java.protectionDomain!!.codeSource.location.toURI())
+        val names = if (location.isDirectory) {
+            location.walkTopDown()
+                .filter { it.isFile && it.name.endsWith(".class") }
+                .map { it.relativeTo(location).invariantSeparatorsPath }
+                .toList()
+        } else {
+            ZipFile(location).use { jar -> jar.entries().asSequence().map { it.name }.filter { it.endsWith(".class") }.toList() }
+        }
+        return names
+            .filter { it.startsWith(APP_PACKAGE) }
+            .map { Class.forName(it.removeSuffix(".class").replace('/', '.'), false, javaClass.classLoader) }
+    }
+
+    /** The app module's directory, which Gradle makes the working directory of its unit tests. */
+    private fun moduleDirectory(): File =
+        generateSequence(File("").absoluteFile) { it.parentFile }
+            .first { File(it, "src/main/AndroidManifest.xml").isFile }
+
+    private companion object {
+        const val APP_PACKAGE = "com/github/e0ipso/tokkl/"
+
+        /** A pattern, and the only files where it may appear. */
+        val SOU

+package com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.api
+
+import java.security.KeyStore
+import java.security.cert.CertificateException
+import java.security.cert.CertificateFactory
+import java.security.cert.X509Certificate
+import javax.net.ssl.TrustManagerFactory
+import javax.net.ssl.X509TrustManager
+import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
+import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
+import org.junit.Assert.assertNotEquals
+import org.junit.Assert.assertThrows
+import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
+import org.junit.Test
+
+/**
+ * The pin check of [PinningTrustManager], with two self-signed certificates
+ * that share a subject (`CN=tokkl.test,O=Tokkl test fixture`) but not a key.
+ * They were made once with
+ *
+ *     openssl req -x509 -newkey ec -pkeyopt ec_paramgen_curve:prime256v1 -nodes -days 36500 \
+ *       -subj '/CN=tokkl.test/O=Tokkl test fixture' -addext 'subjectAltName=DNS:tokkl.test' \
+ *       -keyout <discarded> -out self-signed-<a|b>.crt
+ *
[credential-related line omitted]
+ * only. They end in .crt, since .gitignore keeps every .pem out in case it
+ * holds a key. The platform here is the JDK's default trust manager, which trusts
+ * neither.
+ */
+class PinningTrustManagerTest {
+
+    private val a = certificate("self-signed-a.crt")
+    private val b = certificate("self-signed-b.crt")
+    private val pins = mutableMapOf<String, String>()
+    private var pinLookups = 0
+    private val jdk = trustManager(keyStore = null)
+    private val manager = PinningTrustManager(jdk) { host, port ->
+        pinLookups++
+        pins["$host:$port"]
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun aDifferentCertificateWithTheSameSubjectIsRejected() {
+        assertEquals(a.subjectX500Principal, b.subjectX500Principal)
+        assertNotEquals(ServerCertificate.sha256(a), ServerCertificate.sha256(b))
+        pins["tokkl.test:443"] = ServerCertificate.sha256(a)
+
+        val error = assertThrows(UntrustedCertificateException::class.java) { check(b, "tokkl.test", 443) }
+
+        assertTrue("the prompt says the certificate changed", error.pinChanged)
+        assertEquals(ServerCertificate.of(b, "tokkl.test", 443), error.certificate)
+        assertEquals(B_SHA256, error.certificate.displaySha256)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun thePinnedCertificateIsAccepted() {
+        pins["tokkl.test:443"] = ServerCertificate.sha256(a)
+
+        check(a, "tokkl.test", 443)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun anUnpinnedCertificateFailsWithTheFingerprintAndSubjectForThePrompt() {
+        val error = assertThrows(UntrustedCertificateException::class.java) { check(a, "tokkl.test", 443) }
+
+        assertFalse(error.pinChanged)
+        assertEquals(A_SHA256, error.certificate.displaySha256)
+        assertEquals("O=Tokkl test fixture,CN=tokkl.test", error.certificate.subject)
+        assertEquals("tokkl.test" to 443, error.certificate.host to error.certificate.port)
+        assertTrue("the platform's refusal is the cause", error.cause is CertificateException)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun aPinCoversOnlyItsHostAndPort() {
+        pins["tokkl.test:443"] = ServerCertificate.sha256(a)
+
+        assertThrows(UntrustedCertificateException::class.java) { check(a, "tokkl.test", 8443) }
+        assertThrows(UntrustedCertificateException::class.java) { check(a, "other.test", 443) }
+    }

+    @Test
+    fun withoutTheServersHostThePlatformAloneDecides() {
+        pins["tokkl.test:443"] = ServerCertificate.sha256(a)
+
+        val error = assertThrows(CertificateException::class.java) { manager.checkServerTrusted(arrayOf(a), AUTH_TYPE) }
+
+        assertFalse("no pin applies", error is UntrustedCertificateException)
+        assertEquals(0, pinLookups)
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun aCertificateThePlatformTrustsNeverReachesThePin() {
+        // As if the user had installed the certificate as a CA in Android.
+        val platform = trustManager(KeyStore.getInstance(KeyStore.getDefaultType()).apply {
+            load(null)
+            setCertificateEntry("a", a)
+        })
+        val manager = PinningTrustManager(platform) { _, _ -> error("the pin was read") }
+
+        manager.verify(arrayOf(a), "tokkl.test", 443) { platform.checkServerTrusted(arrayOf(a), AUTH_TYPE) }
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun theFingerprintIsTheOneOpensslPrints() {
+        assertEquals(A_SHA256, ServerCertificate.of(a, "tokkl.test", 443).displaySha256)
+        assertEquals(A_SHA256.replace(":", "").lowercase(), ServerCertificate.sha256(a))
+    }

+            val text = javaClass.getResourceAsStream("/certificates/$name")!!.use { it.readBytes().decodeToString() }
[credential-related line omitted]
+        }
+    }
+
+    /** The JDK's trust manager checks without a host, as Android's does inside a handshake. */
+    private fun check(leaf: X509Certificate, host: String, port: Int) {
+        manager.verify(arrayOf(leaf), host, port) { jdk.checkServerTrusted(arrayOf(leaf), AUTH_TYPE) }
+    }
+
+    private fun certificate(name: String): X509Certificate =
+        javaClass.getResourceAsStream("/certificates/$name")!!.use {
+            CertificateFactory.getInstance("X.509").generateCertificate(it) as X509Certificate
+        }
+
+    private fun trustManager(keyStore: KeyStore?): X509TrustManager =
+        TrustManagerFactory.getInstance(TrustManagerFactory.getDefaultAlgorithm())
+            .apply { init(keyStore) }
+            .trustManagers
+            .filterIsInstance<X509TrustManager>()
+            .single()
+

+    }

+/** A refresh against a MockWebServer whose `GET /api/v9/me` has no `time_resolution_minutes`, as on Toggl Track. */
+class AccountSettingsSourceTest {
+
+    private val server = MockWebServer()
+    private val saved = mutableListOf<AccountSettings>()
+    private lateinit var client: TogglApiClient
+
+    @Before
+    fun setUp() {
+        server.start()
+        val configured = ServerUrl.parse(server.url("/").toString())!!
+        // The shared client sends requests only to the configured server.
+        ConfiguredServer.readStoredFrom { configured }
+        // The user has confirmed the MockWebServer's plain http URL.
+        ConfiguredTrust.readFrom(InMemoryServerTrust(configured))
[credential-related line omitted]
+    }
+
+    @After
+    fun tearDown() {
+        ConfiguredServer.readStoredFrom { null }
+        ConfiguredTrust.readFrom(InMemoryServerTrust())
+        server.close()
+    }
+
+    @Test
+    fun meWithoutTimeResolutionRefreshesWithNoSnapping() {
+        server.enqueue(
+            MockResponse.Builder()
+                .code(200)
+                .addHeader("Content-Type", "application/json")
+                .body("""{"id":7,"email":"ana@example.com","timezone":"Europe/Madrid","beginning_of_week":1}""")
+                .build(),
+        )
+        val stored = AccountSettings("UTC", DayOfWeek.SUNDAY, timeResolutionMinutes = 5.0)
+        // Unconfined runs the refresh to the end inside refresh().
+        val source = AccountSettingsSource(
+            initial = stored,
+            save = { saved += it },
+            client = { client },
+            scope = CoroutineScope(Dispatchers.Unconfined),
+            io = Dispatchers.Unconfined,
+        )
+
+        val refresh = source.refresh()
+
+        assertTrue("the refresh finished", refresh.isCompleted)
+        assertEquals("/api/v9/me", server.takeRequest().url.encodedPath)
+        val expected = AccountSettings("Europe/Madrid", DayOfWeek.MONDAY, timeResolutionMinutes = null)
+        assertEquals(expected, source.state.value)
+        assertEquals(listOf(expected), saved)
+        val start = Instant.parse("2026-10-18T10:10:07Z")
+        val stop = Instant.parse("2026-10-18T10:52:41Z")
+        assertEquals(SnappedSpan(start, stop), source.state.value!!.time()!!.snapUnsent(start, stop))
+    }
+}

+    /**
+     * The shared client with a step that stands in for its trust manager: the
+     * handshake with a server presenting [certificate] fails unless the user
+     * pinned it, as PinningTrustManager makes it fail.
+     */
+    private fun handshakeAsIfServing(certificate: ServerCertificate): OkHttpClient =
+        SharedHttpClient.instance.newBuilder()
+            .addInterceptor { chain ->
+                val pin = trust.pinnedSha256(certificate.host, certificate.port)
+                if (pin != certificate.sha256) {
+                    val refusal = UntrustedCertificateException(certificate, pinChanged = pin != null, cause = CertificateException("no trust anchor"))
+                    throw SSLHandshakeException("handshake failed").apply { initCause(refusal) }
+                }
+                chain.proceed(chain.request())
+            }
+            .build()

 succeeded in 0ms:
premise", ZoneId.of(DEVICE_ZONE), ZoneId.systemDefault())
+        // 04:00 on Sunday 18 October in Los Angeles, and already 01:00 on Monday the 19th in
+        // Kiritimati. The clock carries the device's zone, which the account time ignores.
+        val instant = Instant.parse("2026-10-18T11:00:00Z")
+        val settings = AccountSettings("america/los_angeles", DayOfWeek.MONDAY, timeResolutionMinutes = null)
+
+        val time = settings.time(Clock.fixed(instant, ZoneId.of(DEVICE_ZONE)))!!
+
+        assertEquals(ZoneId.of("America/Los_Angeles"), time.zone)
+        assertEquals(LocalDateTime.of(2026, 10, 18, 4, 0), time.local(instant).toLocalDateTime())
+        assertEquals("04:00", DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("HH:mm").format(time.local(instant)))
+        assertEquals(LocalDateTime.of(2026, 10, 18, 4, 0), time.now().toLocalDateTime())
+        assertEquals(LocalDate.of(2026, 10, 18), time.today())
+        assertEquals(LocalDate.of(2026, 10, 18), LocalDate.now(time.clock))
+        // The week that holds Sunday the 18th starts on Monday the 12th, not on the device's Monday.
+        assertEquals(LocalDate.of(2026, 10, 12), time.startOfWeek(time.today()))
+        assertEquals(ZoneId.of("America/Los_Angeles"), settings.time()!!.clock.zone)
+    }

+    @Test
+    fun snapsUnsentEntriesOnTheAccountZonesGrid() {
+        // An hourly grid in Kolkata (UTC+05:30) falls on the half hour in UTC and in Kiritimati
+        // on the hour. 10:10Z is 15:40 in Kolkata, so the start goes to 16:00 there, 10:30Z.
+        val start = Instant.parse("2026-10-18T10:10:00Z")
+        val stop = Instant.parse("2026-10-18T10:50:00Z")
+        val time = AccountSettings("Asia/Kolkata", DayOfWeek.MONDAY, timeResolutionMinutes = 60.0).time()!!
+
+        val snapped = time.snapUnsent(start, stop)
+
+        assertEquals(SnappedSpan(Instant.parse("2026-10-18T10:30:00Z"), Instant.parse("2026-10-18T11:30:00Z")), snapped)
+    }

+        val time = AccountSettings("Asia/Kolkata", DayOfWeek.MONDAY, timeResolutionMinutes = null).time()!!

+# The proxy's certificate and key, made for this run. HAProxy reads both from
+# one file, as an unprivileged user in its container, so the file is readable
+# by every user. Only this user can enter $work_dir, which holds it, and the
+# cleanup deletes it. The tests get the certificate's fingerprint, never the
+# key.
+proxy_dir="$work_dir/proxy"
+mkdir -m 755 "$proxy_dir"
+(
+    umask 077
+    openssl req -x509 -newkey ec -pkeyopt ec_paramgen_curve:prime256v1 -nodes -days 2 \
+        -subj '/CN=Tokkl instrumented tests' -addext 'subjectAltName=IP:10.0.2.2,IP:127.0.0.1' \
+        -keyout "$work_dir/proxy-key.pem" -out "$proxy_dir/cert.pem" 2>/dev/null
+)
+cat "$proxy_dir/cert.pem" "$work_dir/proxy-key.pem" >"$proxy_dir/server.pem"
+rm -f "$work_dir/proxy-key.pem"
+chmod 644 "$proxy_dir/cert.pem" "$proxy_dir/server.pem"
+tls_fingerprint=$(openssl x509 -in "$proxy_dir/cert.pem" -noout -fingerprint -sha256)
+tls_fingerprint=${tls_fingerprint#*=}
+cat >"$proxy_dir/haproxy.cfg" <<'CONFIG'
+global
+    log stdout format raw local0 warning
+
+defaults
+    mode http
+    log global
+    timeout connect 5s
+    timeout client 30s
+    timeout server 30s
+
+frontend tls
+    bind :8443 ssl crt /usr/local/etc/haproxy/server.pem
+    default_backend tokkl
+
+backend tokkl
+    server tokkl 127.0.0.1:8080
+CONFIG
+chmod 644 "$proxy_dir/haproxy.cfg"
+proxy="$container-tls"
+echo "Starting $PROXY_IMAGE as $proxy"
+docker run --detach --name "$proxy" --network "container:$container" \
+    --volume "$proxy_dir:/usr/local/etc/haproxy:ro" "$PROXY_IMAGE" >/dev/null
+# --cacert trusts this run's certificate alone, so curl checks it as the app does.

+    echo "$0: the TLS proxy did not answer on https://127.0.0.1:$tls_port/healthz. Last proxy output:" >&2
+    docker logs "$proxy" 2>&1 | tail -n 20 >&2
+    exit 1
+fi

package com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.api

/**
 * The one server the app may send requests to (docs/PRD.md principle 1).
 * [SharedHttpClient] refuses every request and every redirect whose scheme,
 * host or port differs from [current].
 */
object ConfiguredServer {

    @Volatile
    private var stored: () -> ServerUrl? = { null }

    @Volatile
    private var signInCandidate: ServerUrl? = null

    private val signInLock = Any()

    /**
     * During sign-in, the URL being tried. Otherwise the stored server. Null
     * when there is neither, which refuses every request.
     */
    val current: ServerUrl?
        get() = signInCandidate ?: stored()

    /** Where [current] reads the stored server from. The app sets this once at start. */
    fun readStoredFrom(source: () -> ServerUrl?) {
        stored = source
    }

    /**
     * Runs [attempt] with [candidate] as the configured server, since sign-in
     * talks to a server before the app has stored it. One attempt runs at a
     * time, and the stored server applies again when it returns.
     */
    fun <T> signingIn(candidate: ServerUrl, attempt: () -> T): T =
        synchronized(signInLock) {
            signInCandidate = candidate
            try {
                attempt()
            } finally {
                signInCandidate = null
            }
        }
}

/**
 * The account the app is signed in to: the configured server and its API token.
[credential-related line omitted]
 *
 * Calls do disk and Keystore I/O, so make them off the main thread.
 */
class Session(
    private val servers: ServerConfigStore,
[credential-related line omitted]
) {

[credential-related line omitted]

    /**
     * Signed in means a server is stored and the token decrypts. A Keystore or
     * disk failure counts as signed out, so the user can sign in again.
     */
    fun isSignedIn(): Boolean =
        try {
            servers.load() != null && tokens.load() != null
        } catch (e: GeneralSecurityException) {
            false
        } catch (e: IOException) {
            false
        }

    /**
     * The API client of the signed-in account, or null when signed out. A Keystore or disk
     * failure counts as signed out, as in [isSignedIn].
     */
    fun client(): TogglApiClient? {
        return try {
            val server = servers.load() ?: return null
            val token = tokens.load() ?: return null
[credential-related line omitted]
        } catch (e: GeneralSecurityException) {
            null
        } catch (e: IOException) {
            null
        }
    }

    /**
     * Stores [server] as the configured server and [token] as its API token.
     * The server is cleared first and written last, so a failure part way
     * leaves the app signed out instead of pairing a token with another server.
     */
    @Throws(GeneralSecurityException::class, IOException::class)
    fun save(server: ServerUrl, token: String) {
        servers.clear()
        tokens.save(token)
        servers.save(server)
    }
}

import androidx.activity.compose.BackHandler
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.runtime.LaunchedEffect
import androidx.compose.runtime.saveable.listSaver
import androidx.compose.runtime.saveable.rememberSaveable
import androidx.compose.runtime.snapshots.SnapshotStateList
import androidx.compose.runtime.toMutableStateList
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalContext
import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.licences.LicencesScreen
import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.session.Session
import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.settings.SettingsScreen
import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.settings.ThemeSettingsModel
import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.signin.SignInModel
import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.signin.SignInScreen
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext

/** The app's screens. */
enum class Destination { SIGN_IN, HOME, SETTINGS, LICENCES }

/**
 * The app's navigation host: a back stack of [Destination]s that survives
 * configuration changes and process death. It starts at sign-in until a
 * server and its token are stored, and at the home screen after that.
 * System back pops the stack and closes the app from its first screen.
 */
@Composable
fun TokklNavHost(signInModel: SignInModel, themeSettings: ThemeSettingsModel) {
    val appContext = LocalContext.current.applicationContext
    val backStack = rememberSaveable(saver = BackStackSaver) { SnapshotStateList<Destination>() }
    if (backStack.isEmpty()) {
        // Shows the window background, which matches the theme, while the
        // stores are read off the main thread.
        LaunchedEffect(Unit) {
            val signedIn = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) { Session(appContext).isSignedIn() }
            backStack.add(if (signedIn) Destination.HOME else Destination.SIGN_IN)
        }
        return
    }
    BackHandler(enabled = backStack.size > 1) { backStack.removeAt(backStack.lastIndex) }
    when (backStack.last()) {
        Destination.SIGN_IN -> SignInScreen(
            model = signInModel,
            onSignedIn = {
                signInModel.reset()
                backStack.clear()
                backStack.add(Destination.HOME)
            },
        )
        Destination.HOME -> LaunchScreen(
            onOpenSettings = { backStack.add(Destination.SETTINGS) },
            onOpenLicences = { backStack.add(Destination.LICENCES) },
        )
        Destination.SETTINGS -> SettingsScreen(
            model = themeSettings,
            onBack = { backStack.removeAt(backStack.lastIndex) },
        )
        Destination.LICENCES -> LicencesScreen(onBack = { backStack.removeAt(backStack.lastIndex) })
    }
}

private val BackStackSaver = listSaver<SnapshotStateList<Destination>, String>(
    save = { stack -> stack.map { it.name } },
    restore = { names -> names.map(Destination::valueOf).toMutableStateList() },
)

class MainActivity : ComponentActivity() {

    private val signInViewModel: SignInViewModel by viewModels()

    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
        enableEdgeToEdge()
        val themeSettings = (application as TokklApplication).themeSettings
        setContent {
            val settings by themeSettings.state.collectAsState()
            val darkTheme = settings.mode.isDark(systemIsDark = isSystemInDarkTheme())
            TokklTheme(darkTheme = darkTheme, dynamicColor = settings.dynamicColor) {
                WindowFollowsTheme(darkTheme)
                TokklNavHost(signInModel = signInViewModel.model, themeSettings = themeSettings)
            }
        }
    }

    /** Coming to the foreground reads the account settings again (docs/PRD.md S7). */
    override fun onStart() {
        super.onStart()
        (application as TokklApplication).accountSettings.refresh()
    }

    /**
     * Paints the window in the scheme's background and picks system bar icons that show on it.
     * The window theme follows a light or dark setting only from Android 12 on, once Android has
     * the app's night mode, and never knows the wallpaper's colours, so this sets the background
     * and the icons from the chosen theme. Effects run before the first frame draws.
     */
    @Composable
    private fun WindowFollowsTheme(darkTheme: Boolean) {
        val background = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.background.toArgb()
        DisposableEffect(darkTheme, background) {
            window.setBackgroundDrawable(background.toDrawable())
            enableEdgeToEdge(
                statusBarStyle = SystemBarStyle.auto(Color.TRANSPARENT, Color.TRANSPARENT) { darkTheme },
                navigationBarStyle = SystemBarStyle.auto(LIGHT_SCRIM, DARK_SCRIM) { darkTheme },
            )
            onDispose {}
        }
    }

    private companion object {
        // enableEdgeToEdge's own navigation bar scrims, which only Android 9 and earlier draw.
        val LIGHT_SCRIM = Color.argb(0xe6, 0xFF, 0xFF, 0xFF)
        val DARK_SCRIM = Color.argb(0x80, 0x1b, 0x1b, 0x1b)
    }
}

/** The home screen until the timer screen replaces it. */

    Scaffold(modifier = modifier.fillMaxSize()) { innerPadding ->

                textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
            )
            Spacer(Modifier.height(16.dp))
            TextButton(onClick = onOpenSettings) {
                Text(stringResource(R.string.settings))
            }
            TextButton(onClick = onOpenLicences) {
                Text(stringResource(R.string.licences))
            }
        }
    }
}

   113	    fun secondsUntilRetry(): Long {
   114	        val retryAt = mutableState.value.retryAt ?: return 0
   115	        val left = retryAt - clock()
   116	        return if (left <= 0) 0 else (left + MILLIS_PER_SECOND - 1) / MILLIS_PER_SECOND
   117	    }
   118	
   119	    /** Validates the form and, when it is complete and nothing blocks it, sends one sign-in request. */
   120	    fun submit() {
   121	        val current = mutableState.value
   122	        if (current.submitting || current.signedIn || secondsUntilRetry() > 0) return
   123	        val server = ServerUrl.parse(current.serverUrl.trim())
   124	        val credentials = credentials(current)
   125	        val errors = inputErrors(current, server)
   126	        if (errors.isNotEmpty() || server == null || credentials == null) {
   127	            mutableState.update { it.copy(inputErrors = errors) }
   128	            return
   129	        }
   130	        mutableState.update { it.copy(inputErrors = emptySet(), submitting = true, failure = null, retryAt = null) }
   131	        scope.launch {
   132	            // A deadline from an earlier process may still be loading.
   133	            restoring.join()
   134	            val stored = deadlines[server.origin]
   135	            if (stored != null && stored > clock()) {
   136	                mutableState.update { it.copy(submitting = false, retryAt = stored) }
   137	                return@launch
   138	            }
   139	            if (stored != null) forget(server.origin)
   140	            val result = withContext(io) { signIn.attempt(server, credentials) }
   141	            val retryAt = when (result) {
   142	                is SignInResult.RateLimited ->

   145	                SignInResult.SignedIn -> null.also { forget(server.origin) }
   146	                else -> null
   147	            }
   148	            mutableState.update { it.after(result, retryAt) }
   149	        }
   150	    }
   151	
   152	    /**
   153	     * The countdown reached zero. Forgets every deadline that has passed, on
   154	     * the phone too.
   155	     */
   156	    fun cooldownEnded() {
   157	        val now = clock()
   158	        deadlines.filterValues { it <= now }.keys.forEach(::forget)
   159	        mutableState.update { it.copy(retryAt = deadlineFor(it.serverUrl)) }
   160	    }
   161

   163	     * The user confirmed the warning about a plain `http://` server. Records
   164	     * the confirmation for that URL, then sends the attempt.
   165	     */
        )
        else -> Unit
    }

package com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.settings

import java.io.IOException
import kotlin.coroutines.CoroutineContext
import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.update
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch
import kotlinx.coroutines.sync.Mutex
import kotlinx.coroutines.sync.withLock
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext

/**
 * The theme settings for the whole process. A change shows in [state] at once, so the theme
 * redraws without a restart, and then goes to disk on [io], one write at a time. When a write
 * fails, [state] goes back to what the disk holds, unless a newer change has replaced it, so the
 * screen never shows a choice that would be gone after a restart.
 *
 * @param initial the stored settings, read before the first frame so the app starts in the
 *   right colours.
 * @param save writes the settings, throwing [IOException] when it cannot.
 * @param scope outlives every screen, so leaving the app does not cancel a pending write.
 */
class ThemeSettingsModel(
    initial: ThemeSettings,
    private val save: (ThemeSettings) -> Unit,
    private val scope: CoroutineScope,
    private val io: CoroutineContext,
) {

    private val mutableState = MutableStateFlow(initial)
    val state: StateFlow<ThemeSettings> = mutableState.asStateFlow()

    private val writes = Mutex()

    /** What the disk holds. Read and written only while holding [writes]. */
    private var saved = initial

    fun onModeChange(mode: ThemeMode) = change { it.copy(mode = mode) }

    fun onDynamicColorChange(on: Boolean) = change { it.copy(dynamicColor = on) }

    private fun change(edit: (ThemeSettings) -> ThemeSettings) {
        mutableState.update(edit)
        scope.launch {
            writes.withLock {
                val wanted = mutableState.value
                if (wanted == saved) return@withLock
                try {
                    withContext(io) { save(wanted) }
                    saved = wanted
                } catch (e: IOException) {
                    mutableState.compareAndSet(wanted, saved)
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

 * The theme settings for the whole process. A change shows in [state] at once, so the theme
 * redraws without a restart, and then goes to disk on [io], one write at a time. When a write
 * fails, [state] goes back to what the disk holds, unless a newer change has replaced it, so the

 *
 * @param initial the stored settings, read before the first frame so the app starts in the
 *   right colours.
 * @param save writes the settings, throwing [IOException] when it cannot.
 * @param scope outlives every screen, so leaving the app does not cancel a pending write.
 */
class ThemeSettingsModel(
    initial: ThemeSettings,
    private val save: (ThemeSettings) -> Unit,
    private val scope: CoroutineScope,
    private val io: CoroutineContext,
) {

    private val mutableState = MutableStateFlow(initial)
    val state: StateFlow<ThemeSettings> = mutableState.asStateFlow()

    private val writes = Mutex()

    /** What the disk holds. Read and written only while holding [writes]. */
    private var saved = initial

    fun onModeChange(mode: ThemeMode) = change { it.copy(mode = mode) }

    fun onDynamicColorChange(on: Boolean) = change { it.copy(dynamicColor = on) }

    private fun change(edit: (ThemeSettings) -> ThemeSettings) {
        mutableState.update(edit)
        scope.launch {
            writes.withLock {
                val wanted = mutableState.value
                if (wanted == saved) return@withLock
                try {
                    withContext(io) { save(wanted) }
                    saved = wanted
                } catch (e: IOException) {
                    mutableState.compareAndSet(wanted, saved)
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
package com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.settings

import android.os.Build

/** Light, dark, or whatever the system uses (docs/PRD.md G1). */
enum class ThemeMode {
    SYSTEM,
    LIGHT,
    DARK,
    ;

    /** Whether the app draws in dark colours, given whether the system is dark now. */
    fun isDark(systemIsDark: Boolean): Boolean =
        when (this) {
            SYSTEM -> systemIsDark
            LIGHT -> false
            DARK -> true
        }
}

/**
 * The theme the user chose on the settings screen. Out of the box the app follows the system
 * and keeps Tokkl's palette. Wallpaper colours are opt-in (docs/PRD.md:77 and G2).
 */
data class ThemeSettings(
    val mode: ThemeMode = ThemeMode.SYSTEM,
    val dynamicColor: Boolean = false,
)

/**
 * Whether the phone has wallpaper colours to offer. Android added them in 12 (API 31), so the
 * settings screen hides the switch below that.
 */
fun dynamicColorAvailable(sdkInt: Int = Build.VERSION.SDK_INT): Boolean = sdkInt >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S
package com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.time

import java.time.DateTimeException
import java.time.ZoneId
import java.time.ZoneOffset

// Tokkl stores any timezone name that Intl.DateTimeFormat accepts (isValidTimeZone in
// ../tokkl/packages/common/time.ts) and returns it from GET /api/v9/me as sent. parseTimeZone
// accepts the same names. The rules here were checked against Node 24.21 (ICU 78.3, tz 2026c).

// Intl's offset syntax: an ASCII or U+2212 minus sign, hours 00 to 23, then optional minutes,
// with or without a colon.
private val OFFSET = Regex("""([+\-\u2212])([01][0-9]|2[0-3])(?::?([0-5][0-9]))?""")

private const val MAX_OFFSET_SECONDS = 18 * 3600

// The names ICU accepts that java.time does not list: the three-letter IDs it keeps from old Java,
// and links tzdb has dropped. Each maps to the zone Intl resolves it to, which for EST, MST and
// HST is a region rather than the fixed offset of ZoneId.SHORT_IDS.
private val INTL_ONLY_NAMES = mapOf(
    "ACT" to "Australia/Darwin",
    "AET" to "Australia/Sydney",
    "AGT" to "America/Buenos_Aires",
    "ART" to "Africa/Cairo",
    "AST" to "America/Anchorage",
    "BET" to "America/Sao_Paulo",
    "BST" to "Asia/Dhaka",
    "CAT" to "Africa/Maputo",
    "CNT" to "America/St_Johns",
    "CST" to "America/Chicago",
    "CTT" to "Asia/Shanghai",
    "Canada/East-Saskatchewan" to "America/Regina",
    "EAT" to "Africa/Nairobi",
    "ECT" to "Europe/Paris",
    "EST" to "America/Panama",
    "GMT+0" to "Etc/GMT+0",
    "GMT-0" to "Etc/GMT-0",
    "HST" to "Pacific/Honolulu",
    "IET" to "America/Indianapolis",
    "IST" to "Asia/Calcutta",
    "JST" to "Asia/Tokyo",
    "MIT" to "Pacific/Apia",
    "MST" to "America/Phoenix",
    "NET" to "Asia/Yerevan",
    "NST" to "Pacific/Auckland",
    "PLT" to "Asia/Karachi",
    "PNT" to "America/Phoenix",
    "PRT" to "America/Puerto_Rico",
    "PST" to "America/Los_Angeles",
    "ROC" to "Asia/Taipei",
    "SST" to "Pacific/Guadalcanal",
    "US/Pacific-New" to "America/Los_Angeles",
    "VST" to "Asia/Saigon",
)

// Every accepted name, lowercased, to the ID ZoneId.of takes. The table above wins over the
// runtime's own list, so those names resolve the same on every tz database. Sorting first keeps
// the result fixed should two IDs ever differ only in case.
private val ZONE_IDS: Map<String, String> by lazy {
    buildMap {
        for ((name, id) in INTL_ONLY_NAMES) put(name.asciiLowercase(), id)
        for (id in ZoneId.getAvailableZoneIds().sorted()) putIfAbsent(id.asciiLowercase(), id)
    }
}

/**
 * The zone for a timezone name from `GET /api/v9/me`, or null when there is none. Never falls back
 * to the device's zone.
 *
 * Accepts what Intl accepts, which is more than [ZoneId.of]: names in any ASCII case (`utc`,
 * `europe/madrid`), ICU's three-letter IDs such as `IST`, and offsets such as `+05`, `+0530` or
 * `-05:00`, also with a U+2212 minus sign. Returns null for what Intl rejects, even where
 * [ZoneId.of] accepts it, such as `Z`, `UT`, `GMT+5` or `UTC+01:00`.
 *
 * Two cases depend on the runtime. A name resolves only if the device's tz database lists it or
 * INTL_ONLY_NAMES maps it. Offsets beyond ±18:00 return null, because [ZoneOffset] stops there
 * while Intl goes to ±23:59.
 */
fun parseTimeZone(name: String): ZoneId? {
    val offset = OFFSET.matchEntire(name)
    if (offset != null) return parseOffset(offset)
    val id = ZONE_IDS[name.asciiLowercase()] ?: return null
    return try {
        ZoneId.of(id)
    } catch (e: DateTimeException) {
        // A tz database without one of the targets in INTL_ONLY_NAMES.
        null
    }
}

private fun parseOffset(match: MatchResult): ZoneOffset? {
    val sign = if (match.groupValues[1] == "+") 1 else -1
    val hours = match.groupValues[2].toInt()
    val minutes = match.groups[3]?.value?.toInt() ?: 0
    val seconds = hours * 3600 + minutes * 60
    if (seconds > MAX_OFFSET_SECONDS) return null
    return ZoneOffset.ofTotalSeconds(sign * seconds)
}

/** Lowercases A to Z only, the case folding Intl uses for timezone names. */
private fun String.asciiLowercase(): String =
    buildString(length) { for (c in this@asciiLowercase) append(if (c in 'A'..'Z') c + 32 else c) }

package com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.session

import android.content.Context
import android.content.SharedPreferences
import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.api.ServerUrl
import java.io.IOException

/**
 * The configured server's base URL, in the app's private preferences. The URL
[credential-related line omitted]
[credential-related line omitted]
 *
 * The first call loads the preferences file, so make calls off the main thread.
 */
class ServerConfigStore(context: Context) {

    private val preferences by lazy { context.getSharedPreferences(FILE_NAME, Context.MODE_PRIVATE) }

    /** The stored server, or null when there is none or it no longer parses. */
    fun load(): ServerUrl? = preferences.getString(KEY_BASE_URL, null)?.let(ServerUrl::parse)

    @Throws(IOException::class)
    fun save(server: ServerUrl) {
        commit { putString(KEY_BASE_URL, server.toString()) }
    }

    @Throws(IOException::class)
    fun clear() {
        commit { remove(KEY_BASE_URL) }
    }

    private fun commit(change: SharedPreferences.Editor.() -> Unit) {
        if (!preferences.edit().apply(change).commit()) throw IOException("Could not write $FILE_NAME")
    }

    internal companion object {
        const val FILE_NAME = "server_config"
        private const val KEY_BASE_URL = "base_url"
    }
}
package com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.api

import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
import org.junit.Test

class ServerUrlTest {

    @Test
    fun hostRootWithAndWithoutTrailingSlash() {
        for (base in listOf("https://example.com", "https://example.com/")) {
            val server = parse(base)
            assertEquals(base, "https://example.com/api/v9/me", server.api("me").toString())
            assertEquals(
                base,
                "https://example.com/reports/api/v3/workspace/1/search/time_entries",
                server.reports("workspace", "1", "search", "time_entries").toString(),
            )
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun basePathWithAndWithoutTrailingSlash() {
        for (base in listOf("https://example.com/tokkl", "https://example.com/tokkl/", "https://example.com/tokkl//")) {
            val server = parse(base)
            assertEquals(base, "https://example.com/tokkl/api/v9/me", server.api("me").toString())
            assertEquals(
                base,
                "https://example.com/tokkl/reports/api/v3/workspace/1/search/time_entries",
                server.reports("workspace", "1", "search", "time_entries").toString(),
            )
        }
    }

    @Test
    fun deeperPathPortAndPlainHttp() {
        val server = parse("http://100.64.0.1:8080/apps/tokkl/")
        assertEquals(
            "http://100.64.0.1:8080/apps/tokkl/api/v9/workspaces/7/time_entries",
            server.api("workspaces", "7", "time_entries").toString(),
        )
        assertEquals(
            "http://100.64.0.1:8080/apps/tokkl/reports/api/v3/workspace/7/summary/time_entries",
            server.reports("workspace", "7", "summary", "time_entries").toString(),
        )
    }

    @Test
    fun emptySegmentsInsideTheBasePathAreDropped() {
        assertEquals("https://example.com/a/b/api/v9/me", parse("https://example.com//a//b/").api("me").toString())
    }

    @Test
    fun encodedBasePathIsKept() {
        assertEquals(
            "https://example.com/my%20tokkl/api/v9/me",
            parse("https://example.com/my%20tokkl/").api("me").toString(),
        )
    }

    @Test
    fun endpointSegmentsCannotAddPathOrQuery() {
        val url = parse("https://example.com/tokkl").api("me/../../admin?x=1")
        assertEquals("https://example.com/tokkl/api/v9/me%2F..%2F..%2Fadmin%3Fx=1", url.toString())
        assertNull(url.query)
    }

    @Test
    fun noEndpointSegmentsGivesTheApiRoot() {
        assertEquals("https://example.com/tokkl/api/v9", parse("https://example.com/tokkl/").api().toString())
    }

    @Test
    fun sameServerWithOrWithoutTrailingSlash() {
        assertEquals(parse("https://example.com/tokkl"), parse("https://example.com/tokkl/"))
        assertEquals("https://example.com/tokkl", parse("https://example.com/tokkl/").toString())
    }

    @Test
    fun rejectsWhatIsNotAnHttpBaseUrl() {
        val bad = listOf(
            "", "example.com", "ftp://example.com", "https://",
            "https://example.com/tokkl?x=1", "https://example.com/#top",
[credential-pattern line omitted]
        )
        for (url in bad) {
            assertNull(url, ServerUrl.parse(url))
        }
    }

    private fun parse(url: String): ServerUrl = ServerUrl.parse(url) ?: error("$url did not parse")
}

package com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.api

import java.io.ByteArrayInputStream
import java.io.DataInputStream
import java.io.File
import java.util.zip.ZipFile
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test

/**
 * Only [SharedHttpClient] may build an HTTP client, so the host guard and the
 * other security settings cover every request. The test reads the constant
 * pool of every compiled app class, so an import alias or a fully qualified
 * name cannot hide a use.
 */
class SingleHttpClientTest {

    private val classes: Map<String, Set<String>> by lazy { appClassReferences() }

    @Test
    fun noAppClassButSharedHttpClientBuildsAnHttpClient() {
        assertTrue("the scan found the app's classes", "com/github/e0ipso/tokkl/MainActivity" in classes)

        val offenders = classes
            .filterKeys { !isSharedHttpClient(it) }
            .flatMap { (name, references) -> references.filter(::isForbidden).map { "$name uses $it" } }

        assertTrue("Use SharedHttpClient.instance instead:\n" + offenders.joinToString("\n"), offenders.isEmpty())
    }

    /** Shows the scan sees a client being built, so the test above cannot pass by finding nothing. */
    @Test
    fun scanSeesTheSharedClientBeingBuilt() {
        val shared = classes.filterKeys(::isSharedHttpClient).values.flatten()

        assertTrue(shared.any { it == "okhttp3/OkHttpClient\$Builder.<init>" && isForbidden(it) })
    }

    private fun isSharedHttpClient(className: String): Boolean =
        className == SHARED || className.startsWith("$SHARED\$")

    private fun isForbidden(reference: String): Boolean =
        FORBIDDEN.any { prefix ->
            reference == prefix ||
                (prefix.endsWith("/") && reference.startsWith(prefix)) ||
                reference.startsWith("$prefix.")
        }

    /** Class name to the classes and members its constant pool refers to, as `owner` or `owner.member`. */
    private fun appClassReferences(): Map<String, Set<String>> {
        val location = File(SharedHttpClient::class.java.protectionDomain!!.codeSource.location.toURI())
        val classFiles: Map<String, ByteArray> = if (location.isDirectory) {
            location.walkTopDown()
                .filter { it.isFile && it.name.endsWith(".class") }
                .associate { it.relativeTo(location).invariantSeparatorsPath.removeSuffix(".class") to it.readBytes() }
        } else {
            ZipFile(location).use { jar ->
                jar.entries().asSequence()
                    .filter { it.name.endsWith(".class") }
                    .associate { it.name.removeSuffix(".class") to jar.getInputStream(it).use { s -> s.readBytes() } }
            }
        }
        return classFiles
            .filterKeys { it.startsWith(APP_PACKAGE) }
            .mapValues { (_, bytes) -> references(bytes) }
    }

    /** Reads the class and member references from a class file's constant pool (JVMS 4.4). */
    private fun references(bytes: ByteArray): Set<String> {
        val input = DataInputStream(ByteArrayInputStream(bytes))
        check(input.readInt() == CLASS_MAGIC) { "not a class file" }
        input.readUnsignedShort()
        input.readUnsignedShort()
        val count = input.readUnsignedShort()
        val utf8 = arrayOfNulls<String>(count)
        val classNameIndex = IntArray(count)
        val nameAndTypeNameIndex = IntArray(count)
        val classEntries = mutableListOf<Int>()
        val memberEntries = mutableListOf<Pair<Int, Int>>()
        var index = 1
        while (index < count) {
            when (val tag = input.readUnsignedByte()) {
                TAG_UTF8 -> utf8[index] = input.readUTF()
                TAG_INTEGER, TAG_FLOAT -> input.readInt()
                TAG_LONG, TAG_DOUBLE -> {
                    input.readLong()
                    index++ // Takes two entries.
                }
                TAG_CLASS -> {
                    classNameIndex[index] = input.readUnsignedShort()
                    classEntries += index
                }
                TAG_STRING, TAG_METHOD_TYPE, TAG_MODULE, TAG_PACKAGE -> input.readUnsignedShort()
                TAG_FIELD_REF, TAG_METHOD_REF, TAG_INTERFACE_METHOD_REF ->
                    memberEntries += input.readUnsignedShort() to input.readUnsignedShort()
                TAG_NAME_AND_TYPE -> {
                    nameAndTypeNameIndex[index] = input.readUnsignedShort()
                    input.readUnsignedShort()
                }
                TAG_METHOD_HANDLE -> {
                    input.readUnsignedByte()
                    input.readUnsignedShort()
                }
                TAG_DYNAMIC, TAG_INVOKE_DYNAMIC -> {
                    input.readUnsignedShort()
                    input.readUnsignedShort()
                }
                else -> error("unknown constant pool tag $tag")
            }
            index++
        }
        val classNames = classEntries.map { utf8[classNameIndex[it]]!! }
        val members = memberEntries.map { (owner, nameAndType) ->
            utf8[classNameIndex[owner]] + "." + utf8[nameAndTypeNameIndex[nameAndType]]
        }
        return (classNames + members).toSet()
    }

    private companion object {
        const val APP_PACKAGE = "com/github/e0ipso/tokkl/"
        const val SHARED = "com/github/e0ipso/tokkl/api/SharedHttpClient"

        /** Ways to build an HTTP client or open a connection without the shared client. */
        val FORBIDDEN = listOf(
            "okhttp3/OkHttpClient\$Builder",
            "okhttp3/OkHttpClient.<init>",
            "okhttp3/OkHttpClient.newBuilder",
            "java/net/URL.openConnection",
            "java/net/URL.openStream",
            "java/net/URLConnection",
            "java/net/HttpURLConnection",
            "javax/net/ssl/HttpsURLConnection",
            "java/net/http/",
            "java/net/Socket.<init>",
            "javax/net/SocketFactory.createSocket",
            "javax/net/ssl/SSLSocketFactory.createSocket",
            "android/webkit/WebView",
            "android/app/DownloadManager",
        )

        const val CLASS_MAGIC = 0xCAFEBABE.toInt()
        const val TAG_UTF8 = 1
        const val TAG_INTEGER = 3
        const val TAG_FLOAT = 4
        const val TAG_LONG = 5
        const val TAG_DOUBLE = 6
        const val TAG_CLASS = 7
        const val TAG_STRING = 8
        const val TAG_FIELD_REF = 9
        const val TAG_METHOD_REF = 10
        const val TAG_INTERFACE_METHOD_REF = 11
        const val TAG_NAME_AND_TYPE = 12
        const val TAG_METHOD_HANDLE = 15
        const val TAG_METHOD_TYPE = 16
        const val TAG_DYNAMIC = 17
        const val TAG_INVOKE_DYNAMIC = 18
        const val TAG_MODULE = 19
        const val TAG_PACKAGE = 20
    }
}
package com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.settings

import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test

class ThemeSettingsTest {

    @Test
    fun defaultsFollowTheSystemWithTokklsPalette() {
        assertEquals(ThemeSettings(mode = ThemeMode.SYSTEM, dynamicColor = false), ThemeSettings())
    }

    @Test
    fun lightAndDarkIgnoreTheSystemAndSystemFollowsIt() {
        for (systemIsDark in listOf(false, true)) {
            assertFalse(ThemeMode.LIGHT.isDark(systemIsDark))
            assertTrue(ThemeMode.DARK.isDark(systemIsDark))
            assertEquals(systemIsDark, ThemeMode.SYSTEM.isDark(systemIsDark))
        }
    }

    /** API 31 is Android 12, the first with wallpaper colours. The app supports API 26 and up. */
    @Test
    fun wallpaperColoursAreOfferedFromAndroid12() {
        for (sdkInt in 26..30) assertFalse("API $sdkInt", dynamicColorAvailable(sdkInt))
        for (sdkInt in 31..37) assertTrue("API $sdkInt", dynamicColorAvailable(sdkInt))
    }
}

import android.view.KeyEvent
import androidx.annotation.StringRes
import androidx.compose.ui.semantics.SemanticsProperties
import androidx.compose.ui.test.assertIsDisplayed
import androidx.compose.ui.test.hasText
import androidx.compose.ui.test.isHeading
import androidx.compose.ui.test.junit4.v2.createAndroidComposeRule
import androidx.compose.ui.test.onNodeWithContentDescription
import androidx.compose.ui.test.onNodeWithText
import androidx.compose.ui.test.performClick
import androidx.test.platform.app.InstrumentationRegistry
import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.MainActivity
import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.R
import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.SessionRule
import com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.awaitText
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Rule
import org.junit.Test

/** The Licences screen, reached from the launch screen of the real activity. */
class LicencesScreenTest {
    @get:Rule(order = 0)
    val session = SessionRule()

    @get:Rule(order = 1)
    val composeRule = createAndroidComposeRule<MainActivity>()

    @Test
    fun launchScreenEntryOpensTheFullOflText() {
        openLicences()

        composeRule.onNodeWithText(string(R.string.licence_anybody_title)).assertIsDisplayed()
        composeRule.onNodeWithText(string(R.string.licence_anybody_licence)).assertIsDisplayed()
        val shown = composeRule.onNode(hasText(OFL_TITLE, substring = true))
            .assertIsDisplayed()
            .fetchSemanticsNode().config[SemanticsProperties.Text]
            .joinToString("") { it.text }
        val shipped = composeRule.activity.resources.openRawResource(R.raw.anybody_ofl)
            .bufferedReader().use { it.readText() }
        // lines() splits at CRLF and LF alike, so this compares content, not line ends.
        assertEquals(shipped.lines(), shown.lines())
    }

    @Test
    fun backButtonReturnsToTheLaunchScreen() {
        openLicences()

        composeRule.onNodeWithContentDescription(string(R.string.back)).performClick()

        composeRule.onNodeWithText(string(R.string.launch_message)).assertIsDisplayed()
    }

    /** System back goes to the launch screen instead of closing the app. */
    @Test
    fun systemBackReturnsToTheLaunchScreen() {
        openLicences()

        composeRule.waitUntil("the app window has input focus", FOCUS_TIMEOUT_MS) {
            var focused = false
            composeRule.activityRule.scenario.onActivity { focused = it.hasWindowFocus() }
            focused
        }
        InstrumentationRegistry.getInstrumentation().sendKeyDownUpSync(KeyEvent.KEYCODE_BACK)

        composeRule.onNodeWithText(string(R.string.launch_message)).assertIsDisplayed()
    }

    /** A rotation, theme or font change recreates the activity. */
    @Test
    fun licencesStayOpenWhenTheActivityIsRecreated() {
        openLicences()

        composeRule.activityRule.scenario.recreate()

        licencesHeading().assertIsDisplayed()
    }

    private fun openLicences() {
        composeRule.awaitText(string(R.string.licences))
        composeRule.onNodeWithText(string(R.string.licences)).performClick()
        licencesHeading().assertIsDisplayed()
    }

    private fun licencesHeading() = composeRule.onNode(hasText(string(R.string.licences)) and isHeading())

    private fun string(@StringRes id: Int): String = composeRule.activity.getString(id)

    private companion object {
        const val OFL_TITLE = "SIL OPEN FONT LICENSE Version 1.1"
        const val FOCUS_TIMEOUT_MS = 10_000L
    }
}

package com.github.e0ipso.tokkl

import android.content.pm.LauncherApps
import android.graphics.drawable.AdaptiveIconDrawable
import android.graphics.drawable.BitmapDrawable
import android.graphics.drawable.ColorDrawable
import android.os.Build
import android.os.Process
import androidx.test.platform.app.InstrumentationRegistry
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertNotNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test

/** The icon a launcher gets for the installed app, through the same API launchers use. */
class LauncherIconTest {

    private val context = InstrumentationRegistry.getInstrumentation().targetContext

    @Test
    fun launcherGetsAnAdaptiveIconForMainActivity() {
        val entries = context.getSystemService(LauncherApps::class.java)
            .getActivityList(context.packageName, Process.myUserHandle())
        assertEquals(listOf(MainActivity::class.java.name), entries.map { it.componentName.className })

        val icon = entries.single().getIcon(0)

        assertTrue("the icon is a ${icon.javaClass.name}", icon is AdaptiveIconDrawable)
        icon as AdaptiveIconDrawable
        // The app's own layers, not a platform wrapper around a legacy icon.
        assertEquals(context.getColor(R.color.ic_launcher_background), (icon.background as? ColorDrawable)?.color)
        val artwork = (context.getDrawable(R.drawable.ic_launcher_foreground) as BitmapDrawable).bitmap
        val foreground = (icon.foreground as? BitmapDrawable)?.bitmap
        assertTrue("the foreground is not Tokkl's artwork", foreground?.sameAs(artwork) == true)
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.TIRAMISU) {
            assertNotNull("no monochrome layer for themed icons", icon.monochrome)
        }
    }
}

/**
 * The Anybody headlines and the text around them at Android's largest font scale, 200%: no
 * text is cut off, leaves the screen or overlaps another. The sign-in form and the settings
 * scroll, so each of their parts has to fit once scrolled into view.
 *
 * The clock and totals styles have no screen yet; ClockAndTotalStylesFitTest covers them.
 */
class LargestFontScaleTest {
    private val composeRule = createAndroidComposeRule<MainActivity>()

    /**
     * The session and the font scale are set before the activity starts, so it never has to
     * restart. Tests start signed in unless marked [SignedOut].
     */
    @get:Rule
    val rules: RuleChain = RuleChain.outerRule(SessionRule())
        .around(SystemFontScale(SystemFontScale.LARGEST))
        .around(composeRule)

    @Test
    fun launchScreenFits() {
        assertEquals(SystemFontScale.LARGEST, composeRule.activity.resources.configuration.fontScale)
        composeRule.awaitText(string(R.string.launch_message))

        assertFitWithoutOverlap(
            text(R.string.app_name),

    @Test
    fun settingsFit() {
        assertEquals(SystemFontScale.LARGEST, composeRule.activity.resources.configuration.fontScale)
        composeRule.awaitText(string(R.string.settings))
        composeRule.onNodeWithText(string(R.string.settings)).performClick()
        composeRule.awaitText(string(R.string.settings_theme))

        val wallpaperColours = dynamicColorAvailable()
        assertFitWhenScrolledTo(
            blocks = listOf(
                "Back button" to composeRule.onNodeWithContentDescription(string(R.string.back)),
                "heading" to composeRule.onNode(hasText(string(R.string.settings)) and isHeading()),
                "theme heading" to composeRule.onNode(hasText(string(R.string.settings_theme)) and isHeading()),
                "follow the system choice" to choice(R.string.settings_theme_system),
                "light choice" to choice(R.string.settings_theme_light),
                "dark choice" to choice(R.string.settings_theme_dark),
            ) + listOfNotNull(
                ("wallpaper colours switch" to switch(R.string.settings_dynamic_colour)).takeIf { wallpaperColours },
            ),
            texts = listOf(
                inside(R.string.settings_theme_system, isSelectable()),
                inside(R.string.settings_theme_light, isSelectable()),
                inside(R.string.settings_theme_dark, isSelectable()),
            ) + if (wallpaperColours) {
                listOf(
                    inside(R.string.settings_dynamic_colour, isToggleable()),
                    inside(R.string.settings_dynamic_colour_summary, isToggleable()),
                )
            } else {
                emptyList()
            },
        )
    }

    private fun field(@StringRes label: Int): SemanticsNodeInteraction =
        composeRule.onNode(hasSetTextAction() and hasText(string(label)))

    /** A radio button, by its label. */
    private fun choice(@StringRes label: Int): SemanticsNodeInteraction =
        composeRule.onNode(hasText(string(label)) and isSelectable())

    private fun switch(@StringRes label: Int): SemanticsNodeInteraction =
        composeRule.onNode(hasText(string(label)) and isToggleable())

    private fun fieldLabel(@StringRes id: Int): Pair<String, SemanticsNodeInteraction> =
        inside(id, hasSetTextAction())

    /** The text node itself, inside a control that merges its children. */
    private fun inside(@StringRes id: Int, control: SemanticsMatcher): Pair<String, SemanticsNodeInteraction> =
        string(id).let { "$it text" to composeRule.onNode(hasText(it) and hasAnyAncestor(control), useUnmergedTree = true) }

    /**
     * Scrolls to each node in turn, which then has to show in full: inside the screen and, for
     * text, not cut off. Then no two [blocks] overlap.
     */
    private fun assertFitWhenScrolledTo(
        blocks: List<Pair<String, SemanticsNodeInteraction>>,
        texts: List<Pair<String, SemanticsNodeInteraction>>,
    ) {
        val screen = composeRule.onRoot().getBoundsInRoot()
        for ((name, node) in blocks + texts) {
            node.performScrollTo()
            node.assertIsDisplayed()
            node.textLayout()?.let { assertFalse("text is cut off: $name", it.didOverflowHeight) }
            val box = node.getUnclippedBoundsInRoot()
            val inside = box.left >= screen.left && box.top >= screen.top &&
                box.right <= screen.right && box.bottom <= screen.bottom
            assertTrue("does not fit on the screen: $name", inside)
        }
        // Unclipped bounds share one scroll position, so they compare even off screen.
        val bounds = blocks.map { (name, node) -> name to node.getUnclippedBoundsInRoot() }
        for ((i, first) in bounds.withIndex()) {
            for (second in bounds.drop(i + 1)) {
                assertFalse("${first.first} overlaps ${second.first}", first.second.overlaps(second.second))
            }
        }
    }

    private fun assertFitWithoutOverlap(vararg nodes: Pair<String, SemanticsNodeInteraction>) {
        val screen = composeRule.onRoot().getBoundsInRoot()
        val bounds = nodes.map { (name, node) ->
            node.assertIsDisplayed()
            node.textLayout()?.let { assertFalse("text is cut off: $name", it.didOverflowHeight) }
            val box = node.getBoundsInRoot()
            val inside = box.left >= screen.left && box.top >= screen.top &&
                box.right <= screen.right && box.bottom <= screen.bottom
            assertTrue("leaves the screen: $name", inside)
            name to box
        }
        for ((i, first) in bounds.withIndex()) {
            for (second in bounds.drop(i + 1)) {
                assertFalse("${first.first} overlaps ${second.first}", first.second.overlaps(second.second))
            }
        }
    }

    /** The text node itself, not the button it may sit in, so its layout result is reachable. */
    private fun text(@StringRes id: Int): Pair<String, SemanticsNodeInteraction> =
        string(id).let { it to composeRule.onNodeWithText(it, useUnmergedTree = true) }

    /**
     * The layout result that semantics hands out is laid out again at the full available width,
     * so its didOverflowWidth is true for any text narrower than its container. Only the height
     * is compared: soft wrapping keeps every line inside the width.
     */
    private fun SemanticsNodeInteraction.textLayout(): TextLayoutResult? {
        if (SemanticsActions.GetTextLayoutResult !in fetchSemanticsNode().config) return null
        val results = mutableListOf<TextLayoutResult>()
        performSemanticsAction(SemanticsActions.GetTextLayoutResult) { it(results) }
        return results.single()
    }

    private fun DpRect.overlaps(other: DpRect): Boolean =
        left < other.right && other.left < right && top < other.bottom && other.top < bottom

    private fun string(@StringRes id: Int): String = composeRule.activity.getString(id)
}
    59	     * signed out. A failed request or write keeps what is stored. While one refresh runs, another
    60	     * call returns that one instead of sending a second request.
    61	     */
    62	    fun refresh(): Job {
    63	        val job = synchronized(lock) {
    64	            refreshing?.takeUnless { it.isCompleted }
    65	                ?: scope.launch(io, start = CoroutineStart.LAZY) { refreshNow() }.also { refreshing = it }
    66	        }
    67	        job.start()
    68	        return job
    69	    }
    70	
    71	    private fun refreshNow() {
    72	        val writesBefore = synchronized(lock) { writes }
    73	        val api = client() ?: return
    74	        val me = api.get(api.server.api("me"), Me.serializer())
    75	        val settings = (me as? ApiResult.Success)?.value?.accountSettings() ?: return
    76	        synchronized(lock) {
    77	            // Sign-in stored newer settings while the request ran.
    78	            if (writes != writesBefore || settings == mutableState.value) return
    79	            try {
    80	                write(settings)
    81	            } catch (e: IOException) {
    82	                // Keeps the stored settings until the next refresh.
    83	            }
    84	        }
    85	    }
    86	
    87	    /** Only while holding [lock]. */
    88	    private fun write(settings: AccountSettings) {
    89	        save(settings)
    90	        writes++
    91	        mutableState.value = settings
    92	    }
    93	}
30:- Client certificates (mTLS).
39:Those servers differ. Some sit behind a reverse proxy with a public certificate, some use a tailnet name with a certificate from `tailscale cert`, some use a self-signed certificate, and some run Tokkl's default: plain HTTP on port 8080.
70:3. **Few requests.** The app refreshes on events (app opened, timer changed, network back), not on a short polling timer. The running clock ticks on the phone from the start time.
71:4. **Tokkl behavior.** Same time parsing, favorites, grouping and undo as the web app, in the account's timezone, so moving between the laptop and the phone needs no relearning.
91:| S2 | Accept HTTPS with a publicly trusted certificate or with a CA the user installed in Android. |
92:| S3 | Accept HTTPS with a self-signed certificate after the user reads a disclaimer and trusts it. The app shows the certificate's SHA-256 fingerprint, trusts that certificate only, and asks again when it changes. It never accepts any certificate without a check. |
93:| S4 | Accept plain `http://` after a warning, stronger than the one in S3, that the token travels unencrypted unless the network encrypts it (Tailscale does). |
96:| S7 | Read the timezone, first day of the week and `time_resolution_minutes` from `GET /api/v9/me`, and use the account timezone everywhere. |
105:| T3 | Show stopped entries as the server returns them, after it snaps them to `time_resolution_minutes`. Entries not yet sent are snapped on the phone with a port of `snapSpan` (`../tokkl/packages/common/snap.ts`) in the account timezone, so their times do not change when the server answers. The running clock is not snapped. |
131:| K1 | Calendar in the account timezone. Phones in portrait show one day at a time, swipe to the next day, and show the week with its daily totals in a strip on top. Landscape, tablets and unfolded foldables show the week. This differs from the web app, which squeezes seven columns of about 45 px into a phone (`../tokkl/packages/web/src/styles.css`, the 760 px media query). |
194:- OkHttp and kotlinx.serialization for the API, with a trust manager that adds the pinned self-signed certificate (S3).
201:Since Android 9 an app cannot use cleartext HTTP unless it opts in, and since Android 7 it trusts only system CAs by default ([network security configuration](https://developer.android.com/privacy-and-security/security-config)). The app's network security config therefore permits cleartext (S4) and trusts user-added CAs (S2). The self-signed certificate in S3 is pinned in the app, separate from Android's trust store.
206:- The app refreshes the running entry and the visible data when it opens, after a change, and when the network comes back. It does not poll on a short timer.
231:- If the build is reproducible, F-Droid can publish the APK signed with the project's own key. `Binaries` points at a versioned APK URL, such as a GitHub release asset, and `AllowedAPKSigningKeys` holds the SHA-256 fingerprint of the signing certificate. F-Droid publishes only when its build matches ([reproducible builds](https://f-droid.org/docs/Reproducible_Builds/)). The docs ask for pinned build-tools and no embedded timestamps.

package com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.api

import java.io.IOException
import kotlinx.serialization.DeserializationStrategy
import kotlinx.serialization.json.JsonElement
import okhttp3.HttpUrl
import okhttp3.MediaType.Companion.toMediaType
import okhttp3.OkHttpClient
import okhttp3.Request
import okhttp3.RequestBody.Companion.toRequestBody
import okhttp3.Response

/**
 * Client for the Toggl Track API v9 and Reports API v3, as served by Tokkl or
 * by Toggl Track. Build endpoint URLs with [server], then call [get], [send]
 * or [sendNoContent]. Calls block the calling thread, so make them off the
 * main thread.
 */
class TogglApiClient(
    val server: ServerUrl,
    private val credentials: ApiCredentials,
    private val httpClient: OkHttpClient = SharedHttpClient.instance,
) {

    fun <T> get(url: HttpUrl, deserializer: DeserializationStrategy<T>): ApiResult<T> =
        send("GET", url, body = null, deserializer)

    /**
     * Sends [body] as JSON, or no body when it is null, and decodes the 2xx
     * response body. An empty body does not decode, so use [sendNoContent]
     * for calls that answer without one. OkHttp refuses a null body for POST,
     * PUT and PATCH.
     */
    fun <T> send(
        method: String,
        url: HttpUrl,
        body: JsonElement?,
        deserializer: DeserializationStrategy<T>,
    ): ApiResult<T> = execute(method, url, body) { decode(it.body.string(), deserializer) }

    /**
     * Like [send], for calls whose response body the caller does not need,
     * such as Tokkl's DELETE of a time entry, task, client or tag, which
     * answer 200 with an empty body. Tokkl's project DELETE answers with the
     * project id as JSON, which this method ignores too. Any 2xx is a success.
     */
    fun sendNoContent(method: String, url: HttpUrl, body: JsonElement? = null): ApiResult<Unit> =
        execute(method, url, body) { ApiResult.Success(Unit) }

    private fun <T> execute(
        method: String,
        url: HttpUrl,
        body: JsonElement?,
        onSuccess: (Response) -> ApiResult<T>,
    ): ApiResult<T> {
        val request = Request.Builder()
            .url(url)
[credential-related line omitted]
            .method(method, body?.let { ApiJson.encodeToString(JsonElement.serializer(), it).toRequestBody(JSON) })
            .build()
        return try {
            httpClient.newCall(request).execute().use { response ->
                if (response.isSuccessful) onSuccess(response) else ApiResult.Failure(httpError(response))
            }
        } catch (e: HostNotAllowedException) {
            ApiResult.Failure(ApiError.HostNotAllowed(e.origin))
        } catch (e: CleartextNotConfirmedException) {
            ApiResult.Failure(ApiError.CleartextNotConfirmed(e.server))
        } catch (e: IOException) {
            ApiResult.Failure(ApiError.Network(e))
        }
    }

    private fun httpError(response: Response): ApiError.Http =
        when (response.code) {
            403 -> ApiError.AuthFailed
            429 -> ApiError.RateLimited(retryAfterSeconds(response.header("Retry-After")))
            else -> ApiError.UnexpectedStatus(response.code)
        }

    private fun <T> decode(text: String, deserializer: DeserializationStrategy<T>): ApiResult<T> =
        try {
            ApiResult.Success(ApiJson.decodeFromString(deserializer, text))
        } catch (e: IllegalArgumentException) {
            // kotlinx.serialization's SerializationException is an IllegalArgumentException.
            ApiResult.Failure(ApiError.InvalidResponse(e))
        }

    private companion object {
        val JSON = "application/json; charset=utf-8".toMediaType()

        /** Tokkl sends whole seconds. An HTTP date or a negative number counts as missing. */
        fun retryAfterSeconds(value: String?): Long? = value?.trim()?.toLongOrNull()?.takeIf { it >= 0 }
    }
}
package com.github.e0ipso.tokkl.api

import java.io.IOException

/** The outcome of one API call. */
sealed interface ApiResult<out T> {
    data class Success<out T>(val value: T) : ApiResult<T>

    data class Failure(val error: ApiError) : ApiResult<Nothing>
}

/** Why an API call failed. */
sealed interface ApiError {

    /** The server answered with a status outside 2xx. */
    sealed interface Http : ApiError {
        val status: Int
    }

[credential-related line omitted]
    data object AuthFailed : Http {
        override val status: Int get() = 403
    }

    /**
[credential-related line omitted]
     * Tokkl. [retryAfterSeconds] is null when `Retry-After` is missing or not
     * a whole number of seconds.
     */
    data class RateLimited(val retryAfterSeconds: Long?) : Http {
        override val status: Int get() = 429
    }

    /** Any other status outside 2xx. */
    data class UnexpectedStatus(override val status: Int) : Http

    /**
     * No HTTP response arrived: no route, DNS failure, refused connection,
     * TLS failure or timeout. Not an [Http] error.
     */
    data class Network(val cause: IOException) : ApiError

    /** A 2xx response whose body is not the expected JSON. */
    data class InvalidResponse(val cause: IllegalArgumentException) : ApiError

    /**
     * The request, or a redirect, pointed at [origin] (`scheme://host:port`),
     * which is not the configured server, so nothing was sent there. Carries
     * no credential.
     */
    data class HostNotAllowed(val origin: String) : ApiError

    /**
     * [server] is a plain `http://` URL the user has not confirmed, so the
     * request, which would have carried the credentials unencrypted, was not
     * sent.
     */
    data class CleartextNotConfirmed(val server: ServerUrl) : ApiError
}
    fun save(server: ServerUrl, token: String, settings: AccountSettings)
}

/**
 * One sign-in attempt: `GET /api/v9/me` with HTTP Basic auth, then the server,
 * the token and the account settings from that response go to [session]. With
[credential-related line omitted]
[credential-related line omitted]
 *
 * The call blocks, so make it off the main thread.
 */
class SignIn(
    private val session: SessionWriter,
    private val httpClient: OkHttpClient = SharedHttpClient.instance,
) {

    fun attempt(server: ServerUrl, credentials: SignInCredentials): SignInResult {
        val client = TogglApiClient(server, credentials.toApiCredentials(), httpClient)
        // Until the server is stored, the URL being tried is the configured server.
        val result = ConfiguredServer.signingIn(server) { client.get(server.api("me"), Me.serializer()) }
        val me = when (result) {
            is ApiResult.Success -> result.value
            is ApiResult.Failure -> return result.error.toSignInResult()
        }
        val settings = me.accountSettings() ?: return SignInResult.NotTokkl
        val token = when (credentials) {
[credential-related line omitted]
[credential-related line omitted]
        }
        return try {
            session.save(server, token, settings)
            SignInResult.SignedIn
        } catch (e: GeneralSecurityException) {
            SignInResult.StorageFailed
        } catch (e: IOException) {
            SignInResult.StorageFailed
        }
    }
}
    emulator_pid=''
    if [ "$status" -eq 137 ]; then
        echo "$0: the emulator was killed with SIGKILL, most likely by the kernel's OOM killer." >&2
        echo "$0: \`journalctl -k --grep=oom\` shows OOM kills. Free memory before running the tests again." >&2
    else
        echo "$0: the emulator exited with status $status. Last emulator output:" >&2
        tail -n 20 "$work_dir/emulator.log" >&2
    fi
}

# ShellCheck 0.11 misses that the EXIT trap runs this.
# shellcheck disable=SC2329
cleanup() {
    local status=$?
    if [ -n "$proxy" ]; then
        docker rm --force --volumes "$proxy" >/dev/null 2>&1 ||
            echo "$0: could not remove container $proxy" >&2
    fi
    if [ -n "$container" ]; then
        docker rm --force --volumes "$container" >/dev/null 2>&1 ||
            echo "$0: could not remove container $container" >&2
    fi
    # sync writes the deletion to disk before an emulator this script booted
    # is killed below. Without it, the file was back on the next boot.
    for device in "${devices_with_credentials[@]}"; do
        adb -s "$device" shell "rm -f $credentials_on_device && sync" >/dev/null 2>&1 ||
            echo "$0: could not remove $credentials_on_device from $device" >&2
    done
    if [ -n "$emulator_pid" ] && ! kill -0 "$emulator_pid" 2>/dev/null; then
        report_emulator_exit
        if [ "$status" -eq 0 ]; then status=1; fi
    fi
    if [ -n "$emulator_pid" ]; then
        adb -s "$emulator_serial" emu kill >/dev/null 2>&1 || kill "$emulator_pid" 2>/dev/null || true
        wait "$emulator_pid" 2>/dev/null || true
    fi
    if [ -n "$emulator_unit" ]; then
        # Stops whatever is left in the scope. It is gone once qemu exits.
        systemctl --user stop "$emulator_unit" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
    fi
    if [ "$adb_was_running" = false ]; then
        adb kill-server >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
    fi
    rm -rf "$work_dir"
    exit "$status"
}
trap cleanup EXIT
trap 'exit 130' INT
trap 'exit 143' TERM

random_hex() {
    od -An -N"$1" -tx1 /dev/urandom | tr -d ' \n'
}

attached_devices() {
    adb devices | awk 'NR > 1 && $2 == "device" { print $1 }'
}

boot_emulator() {
    command -v emulator >/dev/null || { echo "$0: no device attached and emulator is not on PATH" >&2; exit 1; }
    local port=5554 log="$work_dir/emulator.log" deadline=$((SECONDS + 300)) caller
    local -a launch=()
    while adb devices | grep -q "^emulator-${port}[[:space:]]"; do port=$((port + 2)); done
    emulator_serial="emulator-$port"
    if command -v systemd-run >/dev/null && systemctl --user show --property=Version >/dev/null 2>&1; then
        emulator_unit="tokkl-emulator-$port-$(random_hex 4).scope"
        launch=(systemd-run --user --scope --quiet --collect --unit="$emulator_unit" -p OOMPolicy=continue)
        if caller=$(systemctl --user whoami 2>/dev/null); then launch+=(-p "PartOf=$caller"); fi
        launch+=(--)
    fi
    echo "Booting $AVD as $emulator_serial${emulator_unit:+ in $emulator_unit}"

[credential-related line omitted]

The manifest sets `android:allowBackup="false"` and points at `res/xml/backup_rules.xml` (Android 11 and earlier) and `res/xml/data_extraction_rules.xml` (Android 12 and later). Both exclude all nine domains. In the data extraction rules a missing section leaves that transfer mode on for everything, so `cloud-backup`, `device-transfer` and `cross-platform-transfer platform="ios"` each list every domain. A new store therefore needs no backup rules of its own. `BackupRulesTest` (JVM) checks the merged manifest and both files.
